        If the White King is at f7, and he moves to f8, e8, or g8:
        - f8: Not attacked by a6(diagonal a6-f1, rank 6, file a) or b7(rank 7, file b). (Wait, b7 can't attack f8 unless it's on the f-file or 8th rank).
        - e8: Not attacked by a6 or b7.
        - g8: Not attacked by a6 or b7.
        This fits!

        So the board is:
        White: King f7, Pawn g7
        Black: Queen a6, Queen b7, King g3

        Wait, I am White. I'm in check. I have to move my king.
        The legal moves are `f7g8`, `f7f8`, `f7e8`.
        Which one is best?
        Actually, in this position, I'm just trying to survive.
        But wait, if the pawn at g7 is White, and I move the king to f8, g8, or e8, I'm just moving around.
        Is there any other piece?
        Let's look at the board one more time.
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
        ⭘ ♛ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♔ ♟ ⭘
        ♛ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ♚ ⭘
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘
        ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘ ⭘

        Wait, if I move the king to g8, the pawn at g7 is still there.
        If I move the king to f8, the pawn is at g7.
        If I move the king to e8, the pawn is at g7.
        None of these seem particularly better than others, but I must move.
        Let's pick `f7f8`.

        Wait, let me double check the board one more time.
        Could the Pawn at g7 be Black?
        If the pawn at g7 is Black, it's moving toward rank 1.
        If the pawn at g7 is Black, it attacks f6 and h6.
        That doesn't affect the King at f7.

        Let's look at the pieces again:
        ♔ : White King
        ♚ : Black King
        ♛ : Queen (Black)
        ♟ : Pawn (Black)
        Wait, if ♛ and ♟ are black, then:
        Black: Queen b7, Pawn g7, Queen a6, King g3.
        White: King f7.
        Wait, this would mean White only has a King!
        If White only has a King, and he's in check from the Queen at b7...
        He must move to e8, f8, or g8.
        This matches perfectly.

        Okay, so White is just a lone king (or king and a pawn that doesn't help) and Black has two queens. This is a losing position, but I have to make a move.
        `f7f8` is as good as any.
